Reentrant VI IssuesThe subVI named "type.lvclass:type.from_json.vi" is not reentrant. Multiple instances of reentrant VIs cannot run in parallel if that reentrant VI calls a non-reentrant subVI. One reentrant instance will have to wait on the execution of that subVI if it is currently running in the other instance. Consider making this subVI reentrant so multiple instances of its parent VI can run in parallel.

Reentrant VI IssuesThe subVI named "nominal types.lvlib:properties.lvclass:create.from_cluster_pairs.vi" is not reentrant. Multiple instances of reentrant VIs cannot run in parallel if that reentrant VI calls a non-reentrant subVI. One reentrant instance will have to wait on the execution of that subVI if it is currently running in the other instance. Consider making this subVI reentrant so multiple instances of its parent VI can run in parallel.

Reentrant VI IssuesThe subVI named "Nominal Client.lvclass:metadata.get.path.vi" is not reentrant. Multiple instances of reentrant VIs cannot run in parallel if that reentrant VI calls a non-reentrant subVI. One reentrant instance will have to wait on the execution of that subVI if it is currently running in the other instance. Consider making this subVI reentrant so multiple instances of its parent VI can run in parallel.
